Abstract: Multiresolution hash encodings have recently enabled fast and high-fidelity implicit neural representations by storing multi-scale features in fixed-size hash tables along a geometric resolution schedule. However, the standard design is data-agnostic: different resolution levels receive identical hash-table capacity despite large differences in image frequency content. As a result, some levels experience severe hash collisions while others underutilize parameters, leading to inefficient capacity allocation. To address this issue, we propose Collision-Aware Resolution Adaptation (CARA), a method that assigns per-level resolutions by balancing the effective information load across hash levels. This adaptive allocation reduces capacity bottlenecks and improves parameter efficiency. In addition, we introduce an invertible pixel-shuffle transform that reduces hash load factors by redistributing spatial information, thereby mitigating collision-induced information loss without enlarging the hash tables. To support evaluation on extremely high-resolution data, we also curate, to the best of our knowledge, the first uncompressed whole-slide image dataset for academic research. Experiments on Kodak images, gigapixel natural images, and raw whole-slide images demonstrate that CARA consistently improves the fidelity-parameter trade-off. Our method matches state-of-the-art performance while using only $27.76%$ of the parameters, and achieves up to $6.11$ dB PSNR improvement at comparable parameter counts. Code is provided in the supplementary.

</details>


### [70] [Sim-to-Real Traffic Scene Understanding by Decoupling Semantics from Caption Generation with V-JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18562)
*Nguyen Hoai Thuong Bui,Thanh Nguyen Vo,Trinh Tra Giang Nguyen,Ha Duc Bui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦式语义理解框架，先通过V-JEPA与Llama预测器解决结构化交通问答，再用免训练精修机制纠错，最后交由Qwen3-VL-8B生成事件描述，在2026 AI City Challenge Track 2中取得第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该任务要求在合成到真实的域偏移下同时完成VQA与交通事件描述生成；现有视觉-语言方法将语义理解与语言生成纠缠在一起，易产生幻觉，且跨事件阶段推理不一致。

Method: 将语义理解与生成解耦：用冻结的V-JEPA编码器提取预测性场景表示，轻量级Llama预测器回答预定义交通问题得到结构化语义事实；引入免训练的结构化精修机制，利用统计先验、问题间关系与时序事件一致性纠正错误；精修后的事实输入Qwen3-VL-8B生成行人/车辆描述。

Result: 在2026 AI City Challenge Track 2官方基准上，VQA准确率87.09%，总体S2得分60.0853，位列所有参赛队伍第一。

Conclusion: 预测式世界表示结合结构化语义精修，可实现更准确可靠的交通理解，并提升语言生成质量。

Abstract: Track 2 of the AI City Challenge 2026 requires both visual question answering (VQA) and traffic event description generation under a challenging synthetic-to real domain shift. Existing vision-language approaches often entangle semantic understanding with language generation, making them susceptible to hallucination and inconsistent reasoning across event phases. In this work, we propose a decoupled semantic understanding framework that first resolves predefined traffic questions into structured semantic facts and subsequently uses these facts to guide caption generation. A frozen V-JEPA encoder extracts predictive scene representations, while a lightweight Llama-based predictor produces answers for VQA queries. To improve reliability, we introduce a training-free structured refinement mechanism that exploits statistical priors, inter-question relationships, and temporal event consistency to correct prediction errors. The refined semantic facts are then provided to Qwen3-VL-8B to generate pedestrian and vehicle descriptions for each traffic event. Experimental results on the official 2026 AI City Challenge Track 2 benchmark show that the proposed method achieves 87.09% VQA accuracy and an overall S2 score of 60.0853, ranking first among all participating teams. These results demonstrate that predictive world representations combined with structured semantic refinement enable more accurate and reliable traffic understanding, leading to higher-quality lan guage generation.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Where to Focus: Self-Supervised Multi-Scale ViTs for Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18578)
*Anabel Stammer,Valay Bundele,Mehran Hosseinzadeh,Hendrik P. A. Lensch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRAFT框架，用自监督注意力自适应分配空间分辨率，以粗到细的混合尺度表示提升病理ViT性能并降低推理计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理诊断先定位可疑组织再放大观察，证据稀疏且跨多个生物尺度；而现有自监督ViT对所有区域统一token化，未按诊断需求分配空间分辨率。已病理基础模型多靠扩大数据和容量提升表示，仍保留均匀tokenization，因此作者探究能否通过"学习在哪里分配空间分辨率"来改进表示。

Method: 提出CRAFT（Coarse-to-fine Region-Adaptive Feature Tokenization），基于DINO框架，利用自监督注意力选择性细化有信息量的区域、同时保留粗粒度上下文，学习图像相关的混合尺度表示；并引入对称跨尺度正则化目标，促使粗、细表示互补。

Result: 在CAMELYON16、TCGA-Lung亚型分类和TCGA-LUAD生存预测三个任务上，CRAFT一致优于同规模自监督方法，且推理计算更低；仅用22M参数骨干和相对较小的病理数据集训练，即可媲美甚至超越更大的病理基础模型。

Conclusion: 学习动态分配空间分辨率的混合尺度tokenization是提升病理自监督表示的有效方向，能在更低推理成本和更小模型规模下取得竞争性乃至更优性能。

Abstract: Pathologists diagnose diseases by first locating suspicious tissue and then examining it at higher magnification, whereas self-supervised vision transformers (ViTs) allocate the same spatial resolution to every image region despite diagnostic evidence being sparse and spanning multiple biological scales. Recent pathology foundation models have substantially improved representation quality by scaling training data and model capacity, but largely retain uniform tokenization. We instead investigate whether pathology representations can be improved by learning where to allocate spatial resolution during self-supervised learning. To this end, we propose CRAFT (Coarse-to-fine Region-Adaptive Feature Tokenization), a DINO-based framework that learns image-dependent mixed-scale representations by using self-supervised attention to selectively refine informative regions while preserving coarse context, together with a symmetric cross-scale regularization objective that encourages complementary coarse and fine representations. Across CAMELYON16, TCGA-Lung subtype classification, and TCGA-LUAD survival prediction, CRAFT consistently outperforms comparable-scale self-supervised methods while requiring lower inference computation. Despite using only a compact 22M parameter backbone trained on comparatively small pathology datasets, CRAFT remains competitive with, and often surpasses, substantially larger pathology foundation models.

</details>


### [72] [On-the-Fly Homographies Calibration for Multi-Camera Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18582)
*David Voihanski,Mor Sinai,Ben Zion Bobrovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种"即时"几何自校准的多相机跟踪系统：以粗略人工单应矩阵为起点，利用实时检测元数据持续优化地平面几何，在隐私受限、带宽受限、无法离线标定的大规模场景中实现零额外延迟、跨区域一致的鸟瞰图跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统精确多相机跟踪依赖严格的3D现场标定，但大规模部署中该要求往往在操作上不可行：隐私法规常禁止录制视频用于离线标定；带宽有限，无法同步数百路高清视频流；在巨大物理场地上布设标定目标在后勤上不可行。

Method: 以粗略人工单应矩阵为初始值，提出基于质心的投影优化（PO），利用实时检测流异步地持续对齐地平面几何；PO仅作用于已传输的轻量级元数据，因而对实时跟踪器不引入任何计算延迟；优化后的几何输入多相机鸟瞰图（BEV）跟踪器，融合检测并跨区域统一轨迹。

Result: 系统可在无人工干预下自动适应相机移动或环境变化，并对跟踪器保持零计算开销；结合BEV跟踪实现跨区域的检测融合与轨迹统一，在无法进行静态录制视频标定的动态环境中维持全局一致性。

Conclusion: 仅依赖实时匿名元数据的方案构成隐私安全、零开销且具韧性的跟踪流水线，为大规模、隐私受限、带宽受限场景下的多相机跟踪提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Precise multi-camera tracking traditionally relies on rigorous 3D site calibration, yet this requirement is often operationally impossible in large-scale deployments. Privacy regulations frequently prohibit recording video for offline calibration; limited bandwidth precludes synchronizing high-resolution streams from hundreds of cameras; and covering immense physical sites with calibration targets is logistically infeasible. We present a multi-camera homography calibration system designed to overcome these barriers through "on-the-fly" geometric refinement. Starting from coarse manual homographies, we introduce a centroid-based projection optimization (PO) that continuously aligns the ground-plane geometry using live detection streams. Because PO operates asynchronously on already-transmitted, lightweight metadata, it adds zero computational latency to the real-time tracker. This allows the system to adapt automatically to camera movements or environmental changes without human intervention. This optimized geometry feeds a multi-camera bird's-eye-view (BEV) tracker that fuses detections and unifies trajectories across zones. Crucially, by operating strictly on live anonymous metadata, our solution ensures a privacy-safe, zero-overhead, and resilient tracking pipeline that maintains global consistency in dynamic environments where static, recorded-video calibration is impossible.

</details>


### [73] [PULSE: Unlocking Practical Image Compression on Single-Thread CPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18602)
*Zhaoyang Jia,Tianyu Zhang,Zihan Zheng,Wenxuan Xie,Jiahao Li,Bin Li,Houqiang Li,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PULSE 是一个实用图像编解码器，能在 CPU 等资源受限硬件上实现低延迟解码与高效熵编码，并通过由启发式探针引导的智能体进化流程提升压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式图像压缩方法在资源受限硬件（尤其是 CPU）上计算开销高昂，难以实用。

Method: 提出 PULSE：采用超低复杂度 5.2 kMAC/pixel 神经接收器实现多平台低延迟解码；使用整数线性 CDF 预测器和 meta prior 实现比特精确的高效熵编码；并引入由启发式探针引导、人机（人类-LLM）协作的智能体进化流程迭代优化架构。

Result: 单 CPU 线程解码 1080p 图像仅需 126 ms，压缩性能与 HM 相当；经感知优化后可与 MS-ILLM 等更大的感知编解码器竞争。

Conclusion: PULSE 在严格计算预算下实现了实用且高效的图像压缩，在 CPU 上兼具低延迟与有竞争力的压缩/感知性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in learned image compression, existing methods remain computationally expensive on resource-constrained hardware, particularly CPUs. We introduce PULSE, a practical codec that enables (1) low-latency decoding on diverse hardware platforms with an ultra-low-complexity 5.2 kMAC/pixel neural receiver, and (2) efficient bit-exact entropy coding with an integer linear CDF predictor and a meta prior. To recover compression performance under this tight budget, we introduce an agentic evolution process guided by heuristic probes that iteratively improves the architecture through human-LLM collaboration. PULSE decodes a 1080p image in 126 ms on a single CPU thread while achieving compression performance comparable to HM. After perceptual optimization, PULSE competes with larger perceptual codecs like MS-ILLM. Codes are at https://github.com/microsoft/GenCodec/tree/main/PULSE

</details>


### [74] [FIVE-VLA: Fast and EffectIVE Autonomous Driving with Recurrent Action Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18623)
*Kemal Oksuz,Alexandru Buburuzan,Yuhan Yao,Puneet K. Dokania*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FIVE-VLA是一种轻量高效的自动驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型，通过高效视觉编码器与递归动作记忆模块，实现高分辨率输入下的低token、无文本生成轨迹预测，在参数、性能与推理速度上全面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶VLA模型存在三大问题：参数量过大、高分辨率图像处理效率低、缺乏时间记忆（无法建模时序上下文），限制了其在闭环驾驶与边缘设备上的应用。

Method: 提出FIVE-VLA，包含两项核心贡献：(1) 高效视觉编码器，处理448×896高分辨率图像仅生成98个token（比现有方法少5倍以上），并完全绕过文本生成，直接单次前向预测轨迹；(2) Recurrent Action Memory (RAM)，一个轻量模块，以先前动作token为条件进行动作预测，提供对超车、紧急制动等操作至关重要的时序上下文。

Result: 模型仅641M参数。在Bench2Drive闭环驾驶基准上，无交通违规完成路线的比例比先前SOTA VLA高约10%。在大规模真实世界NVIDIA Physical AI AV数据集的非反应式开环仿真中，单视角和四视角设置下碰撞违规率分别比SimLingo低10.2%和7.7%。推理速度为A100上约30 fps、T4（边缘设备代理）上约4 fps，相比先前方法提速8-30倍。

Conclusion: FIVE-VLA通过高效视觉编码与时序记忆机制，在保持轻量参数的同时提升了驾驶安全性与合规性，并显著提高推理效率，适用于实际部署尤其边缘设备。

Abstract: State-of-the-art vision-language-action models (VLA) for autonomous driving face critical limitations: excessive parameter counts, inefficient high-resolution image processing, and lack of temporal memory. We introduce Fast and EffectIVE VLA (FIVE-VLA) to address these through two key contributions. First, we employ an efficient vision encoder that processes high-resolution ($448 \times 896$) images while generating only 98 tokens, over $5\times$ fewer than existing approaches, and bypass text generation entirely for single-pass trajectory prediction. Second, we propose Recurrent Action Memory (RAM), a lightweight module that conditions action prediction on previous action tokens, providing temporal context critical for manoeuvres such as overtaking and emergency braking. With only 641M parameters, FIVE-VLA completes $\sim$10% more routes without traffic rule infractions than the previous state-of-the-art VLA on the challenging Bench2Drive closed-loop driving benchmark. Non-reactive open-loop simulation on the large-scale real-world NVIDIA Physical AI AV dataset shows 10.2% and 7.7% lower collision-violation rates than SimLingo in single- and four-view settings, respectively. Additionally, FIVE-VLA runs at $\sim$30 fps on an A100 and $\sim$4 fps on a T4 GPU (proxy to an edge device), representing an 8-30$\times$ speedup over previous methods.

</details>


### [75] [VibeAvatar: Aligning Phonetic Kinematics and Human Aesthetics for High-Fidelity Talking Avatar Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18632)
*Qilin Wang,Mingyu Li,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VibeAvatar框架，通过在不同阶段解耦口型精度与运动美学两个目标，实现高保真、高美学、高效率的说话头像合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法难以同时兼顾准确的口型、符合人类偏好的运动美学以及高效推理。作者观察到语音准确性与运动美学来源根本不同，应由互补阶段分别处理，而非由单一生成器隐式学习。

Method: VibeAvatar在两个阶段解耦上述目标：条件阶段使用语音运动学适配器（PKA），将面向识别的语音特征转换为语音-运动学条件；后训练阶段使用美学运动策略（AMP），通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）优化流一致性随机采样策略。运动生成器为轻量级流模型，工作在紧凑的1D warped潜空间。

Result: 在客观指标与用户研究中，VibeAvatar在口型、美学和效率上均达到最新水平；生成10秒512px视频耗时低于10秒，显存占用仅约3GB。

Conclusion: 将语音精度与运动美学在互补阶段显式解耦，是同时提升说话头像合成质量与效率的有效思路。

Abstract: Multi-modal talking avatar synthesis aims to generate realistic talking videos from a reference portrait and speech. Despite rapid progress in diffusion-based methods, existing approaches still struggle to jointly achieve accurate lip articulation, human-preferred motion aesthetics, and efficient inference. We observe that phonetic accuracy and motion aesthetics arise from fundamentally different sources and should be addressed at complementary stages rather than learned implicitly by a single generator. Based on this insight, we propose VibeAvatar, which disentangles these two objectives through a Phonetic Kinematics Adapter (PKA) that converts recognition-oriented speech features into phonetic-kinematic conditions at the conditioning stage, and an Aesthetic Motion Policy (AMP) that optimizes a flow-consistent stochastic sampling policy via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) at the post-training stage. With a lightweight flow-based motion generator operating in a compact 1D warp-based latent motion space, VibeAvatar achieves state-of-the-art results in articulation, aesthetics, and efficiency on both objective metrics and user studies, while generating a 10-second 512px video in under 10 seconds with only $\sim$3GB VRAM.

</details>


### [76] [GenStream: Semantic Streaming Framework for Generative Reconstruction of Human-centric Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18634)
*Emanuele Artioli,Daniele Lorenzi,Shivi Vats,Farzad Tashtarian,Christian Timmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenStream 提出一种语义流媒体框架，用骨架关键点、相机视角参数和静态3D背景模型等结构化元数据替代稠密视频帧，在客户端生成式重建人体并合成场景，实现相较 HEVC 超过 99.9% 的带宽缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频编解码器对前景与背景像素统一重编码，忽略场景语义结构，在背景静态、运动集中于少数显著主体的场景（如体育、表演、交互媒体）中造成严重带宽浪费。

Method: 将每个场景编码为骨架关键点、相机视角参数与静态3D背景模型的组合并传输至客户端，由生成式模型重建照片级真实人体，并按原始视角合成到3D场景中。

Result: 在奥运花样滑冰素材上进行了部分验证，展示出在极小数据量下实现高感知保真度的潜力；连续数据流带宽相较 HEVC 降低超过 99.9%。

Conclusion: GenStream 为体素化虚拟人合成、跨视角规范化3D演员融合及个性化观看体验开辟了新方向，为后编解码器时代的可扩展智能流媒体奠定基础，但存在客户端计算开销大与泛化性挑战。

Abstract: Video streaming dominates global internet traffic, yet conventional pipelines remain inefficient for structured, human-centric content such as sports, performance, or interactive media. Standard codecs re-encode entire frames, foreground and background alike, treating all pixels uniformly and ignoring the semantic structure of the scene. This leads to significant bandwidth waste, particularly in scenarios where backgrounds are static and motion is constrained to a few salient actors. We introduce GenStream, a semantic streaming framework that replaces dense video frames with compact, structured metadata. Instead of transmitting pixels, GenStream encodes each scene as a combination of skeletal keypoints, camera viewpoint parameters, and a static 3D background model. These elements are transmitted to the client, where a generative model reconstructs photorealistic human figures and composites them into the 3D scene from the original viewpoint. This paradigm enables extreme compression, achieving over 99.9% bandwidth reduction compared to HEVC for the continuous data stream. We partially validate GenStream on Olympic figure skating footage and demonstrate potential for high perceptual fidelity under minimal data. While acknowledging the significant computational costs shifted to the client and challenges in generalization, GenStream opens new directions in volumetric avatar synthesis, canonical 3D actor fusion across views, and personalized viewing experiences, laying the groundwork for scalable, intelligent streaming in the post-codec era.

</details>


### [77] [Video-Based Markerless Motion Capture for Clinical and Rehabilitation Biomechanics: A PRISMA-ScR Scoping Review of Validated Architectures, Clinical Readiness, and Emerging Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18667)
*Florian Delaplace,Elodie Piche,Frédéric Chorin,Raphael Zory*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一项范围综述纳入117项研究，发现基于视频的无标记动作捕捉在健康成人步行的矢状面下肢关节角度误差约为5–6°，尚未达到临床可接受水平，且在病理与老年人群、面外运动学和动力学方面验证严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视频的无标记动作捕捉有望摆脱光电系统的成本、空间与皮肤标记限制，在临床与康复场景中具有潜力；但现有验证流程能否达到临床可接受的生物力学精度、以及与底层计算机视觉研究的关系尚不明确。

Method: 遵循PRISMA范围综述扩展，注册方案并检索PubMed、Scopus与IEEE Xplore（2015年1月至2026年2月；计算机视觉检索更新至2026年7月）。采用双层设计：主语料为经过验证的生物力学研究，辅以一个精选但刻意非穷尽的计算机视觉新兴研究语料并作定性使用；提取研究特征、流程架构、验证方法与关节角度精度。

Result: 共纳入117项研究，多为2024年以后发表，且以实验室环境下健康成人步行研究为主。流程分为跨单目与多相机模态的五类架构家族，多数仅报告原始关节角度而未做生物力学精化。矢状面下肢一致性集中在5–6°左右，通常未达临床可接受标准；面外运动学、动力学以及病理或老年人群极少被验证。新兴计算机视觉构件（基础模型网格恢复、可微逆运动学、基于视频的动力学）在验证研究中几乎缺席。

Conclusion: 基于视频的无标记捕捉在临床关节运动学上尚不能替代基于标记的系统，且在康复最需要的人群与指标（老年与病理人群、面外运动学、动力学）上验证不足。将这一证据缺口与新兴计算机视觉进展相映射，作者提出用于生成假设的设计指南（而非已验证方法），以引导下一代流程走向可及且具临床意义的运动分析。

Abstract: Background.. Video-based markerless motion capture promises movement analysis without the cost, space and skin-marker constraints of optoelectronic systems, with particular potential for clinical and rehabilitation settings. Whether validated pipelines yet deliver clinically acceptable biomechanics, and how they relate to the underlying computer-vision research, remains unclear. Methods. We conducted a scoping review following the PRISMA extension for Scoping Reviews, with a registered protocol and searches of PubMed, Scopus and IEEE Xplore (January 2015 to February 2026; the computer-vision scan was updated to July 2026). A dual-tier design paired a primary corpus of validated biomechanical studies with a complementary, curated and deliberately non-exhaustive corpus of emerging computer-vision work, used qualitatively. We charted study characteristics, pipeline architecture, validation methods and joint-angle accuracy. Results. We included 117 studies, most published from 2024 onward and conducted on healthy adults walking in a laboratory. Pipelines formed five architectural families across monocular and multi-camera modalities; most reported raw joint angles without biomechanical refinement. Sagittal lower-limb agreement clustered around 5 to 6{\textdegree}, generally short of clinical acceptability, while out-of-plane kinematics, kinetics, and pathological or older populations were rarely validated. Emerging computer-vision building blocks (foundation-model mesh recovery, differentiable inverse kinematics, video-based kinetics) were almost absent from validated studies. Conclusions. Video-based markerless capture is not yet interchangeable with marker-based systems for clinical joint kinematics, and it remains barely validated where rehabilitation needs it most: older and pathological populations, out-of-plane kinematics, and kinetics. Mapping this evidence gap onto emerging computer-vision advances, we propose hypothesis-generating design guidelines, not a validated method, to steer the next generation of pipelines toward accessible, clinically meaningful movement analysis.

</details>


### [78] [Generalist-Specialist Mixture-of-Experts for Rare Pathology Detection in Multimodal Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18688)
*Johannes Kaiser,Florian Braunmiller,Daniel Rückert,Georgios Kaissis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GS-MoE双分支架构，通过跨模态通用模型与模态专属专家结合实现域约束特征融合，在RadImageNet上恢复了对六种低流行度病理的检测，且推理时激活参数减少约53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态医学影像AI模型需在模态特异性专精与跨模态共享表示之间取得平衡，而纯Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）架构无法满足这一权衡：基于专家的路由虽提升域内学习，却可能牺牲对罕见（低流行度）病理尤为关键的跨模态信号。

Method: 提出Generalist-Specialist-MoE（GS-MoE），一种双分支MoE架构，将跨模态通用模型（generalist）与多个模态专属专家（specialists）通过域约束特征融合（domain-constrained feature fusion）相结合。

Result: 在RadImageNet（135万张图像、165种病理、三种模态）上，GS-MoE恢复了六种低流行度病理的检测，这些病理在所有基线模型上F1均为0，单类提升最高达+0.60 F1；同时聚合指标MCC达0.770，略超稠密模型与仅专家MoE基线，且推理时激活参数比最强的稠密模型少约53%。

Conclusion: 双分支的通用-专家MoE架构配合域约束特征融合，能够在保持甚至略微提升整体聚合性能的同时，显著改善罕见病理的跨模态检测能力，并大幅降低推理计算开销。

Abstract: AI models for multimodal medical imaging must balance modality-specific specialization with cross-modal shared representations, a trade-off that pure Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures currently fail to satisfy. Expert-based routing improves in-domain learning but may sacrifice cross-modal signals, which appear particularly important for rare (low-prevalence) pathologies in our experiments. To resolve this, we introduce Generalist-Specialist-MoE (GS-MoE), a two-branch (MoE) architecture that couples a cross-modal generalist model with distinct modality-specific specialists (experts) via domain-constrained feature fusion. On RadImageNet (1.35M images, 165 pathologies, three modalities), GS-MoE recovers detection of six low-prevalence pathologies on which every baseline scores F1 $=$ 0, with per-class gains up to +0.60 F1. It attains this while even slightly exceeding dense and specialist-only MoE aggregate baselines (MCC 0.770), while using ${\sim}53\%$ fewer active parameters at inference than the strongest investigated dense model.

</details>


### [79] [RankGround: Efficient High-Resolution GUI Grounding via Lightweight Reranker-Guided Crop Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18690)
*Liyang Fan,Xinping Bi,Yitai Li,Shuaimin Li,Hui Li,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RankGround 两阶段框架，用单次 VLM 调用实现精准 GUI grounding，推理提速 1.4×，平均定位精度提升 5.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI grounding 方法在精度与效率间存在根本权衡：整图直接推理难以捕捉小而相似的 UI 元素，多裁剪策略虽提升定位却需多次昂贵的 VLM 调用。

Method: 两阶段框架：核心为轻量多模态重排序器 GroundRanker，从密集候选裁剪中选出最优裁剪，从而只需单次 VLM 调用。因无现成排序数据集，从已有 grounding 数据集构建排序监督数据；采用严格包含准则与边界感知正样本增强以提升对齐与杂乱布局下的空间覆盖；训练采用两阶段课程，先以 pointwise 目标学习粗粒度包含关系，再用 listwise 目标区分视觉相似裁剪间的细微语义与空间差异。

Result: 在全部骨干网络与屏幕尺度上一致优于强基线：推理速度提升 1.4 倍，定位精度较次优方法平均提升 5.5%。

Conclusion: RankGround 在效率与精度两方面均达到 GUI grounding 的新 SOTA，验证了"候选重排序 + 单次 VLM 调用"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding is a fundamental perception task for multimodal agents, enabling them to interpret natural language instructions and interact with digital interfaces. Existing methods face a fundamental trade-off between accuracy and efficiency: direct full-image inference often fails to capture small or visually similar UI elements, while multi-crop strategies improve localization at the cost of multiple expensive Vision-Language Model (VLM) calls per query.
  To address this challenge, we propose RankGround, a two-stage framework that achieves accurate GUI grounding with a single VLM call per query. Central to our approach is GroundRanker, a lightweight multimodal reranker that identifies the most promising crop from a dense candidate set. Because no off-the-shelf ranking dataset is available, we construct ranking supervision data from existing grounding datasets. A strict containment criterion and boundary-aware positive augmentation improve alignment and spatial coverage in cluttered layouts. GroundRanker is then trained with a two-stage curriculum: a pointwise objective first learns coarse containment, and a listwise objective refines subtle semantic and spatial distinctions among visually similar crops.
  Experimental results show that RankGround consistently outperforms strong baselines while reducing computational cost. It achieves 1.4 times faster inference and improves localization accuracy by 5.5% on average over the second-best method across all backbones and screen scales, establishing a new state of the art in both efficiency and precision for GUI grounding.

</details>


### [80] [Mask IPL: Noise-Free Intrinsic Position Learning via Computation Graph Clipping for Event-Based Spike-Driven Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18716)
*Yimeng Shan,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了脉冲神经网络事件跟踪中Intrinsic Position Learning (IPL)的有效性机制，指出IPL在前向与反向传播中引入噪声，并提出无额外参数的计算图裁剪方法Mask IPL予以消除，在多个数据集上提升AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN契合事件相机的事件驱动特性，IPL作为事件驱动跟踪中主流的位置编码方式，效果显著但缺乏系统性理论分析，其有效性机理不明，且存在未被发现的噪声问题。

Method: 系统分析IPL，论证其有效性来源于IPL与多阶段卷积的协同作用：联合张量中的零块充当卷积零填充，边界效应逐层传播，使每次参数更新都能感知模板与搜索帧的相对位移。进而提出Computation Graph Clipping方法，依据布局确定有效性掩码并施加于每层操作，使无效区域在前向与反向传播中等价于零填充，从而在不引入额外参数的前提下消除噪声，得到Mask IPL。

Result: 在不增加参数和计算开销的情况下，Mask IPL提升了Tiny-scale跟踪器在FE108、FELT和VisEvent上的AUC，并对Base-scale跟踪器也取得一致提升。

Conclusion: IPL的有效性源于其与多阶段卷积的协同产生的相对位移梯度信息，而其前向与反向噪声会增大推理误差并阻碍参数收敛；Mask IPL通过计算图裁剪消除噪声，使实际梯度与理想梯度一致，代价为零额外参数与计算。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) match the event-driven nature of event cameras and naturally extract spatiotemporal features. These properties have motivated a series of recent studies on event-based tracking with SNNs. Intrinsic Position Learning (IPL) acquires strong position information without introducing additional parameters, making it a mainstream approach for position encoding in event-based spike-driven tracking. However, the mechanism behind its effectiveness lacks systematic theoretical analysis. Moreover, our analysis reveals that IPL introduces noise in both forward and backward propagation. The former increases inference error, while the latter prevents parameters from converging to better solutions. This paper presents a systematic analysis of IPL and demonstrates that its effectiveness stems from the synergy between IPL and multi-stage convolution. The zero blocks in the joint tensor act as zero padding for convolution, and the resulting boundary effect propagates layer by layer through multi-stage convolution. Every parameter update is therefore driven by a gradient that perceives the relative displacement between template and search frames. Positional encoding added after the convolutional stage cannot provide this information. We further propose a simple Computation Graph Clipping method that applies a validity mask determined by the layout to the operations of every layer, making invalid regions equivalent to zero padding in both forward and backward propagation. This eliminates the noise without introducing additional parameters and makes the actual gradient coincide with the ideal gradient. We name the improved method Mask IPL. Without increasing parameters or computational cost, Mask IPL improves the AUC of the Tiny-scale tracker on FE108, FELT, and VisEvent, and consistently improves the Base-scale tracker as well.

</details>


### [81] [Geometry beneath the Waves: Dense Priors for Sparse-View Underwater 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18737)
*Harvey Caldeira,Haoran Wang,Guoxi Huang,Shaoyu Cai,Rachel Fu,Nantheera Anantrasirichai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 水下3D重建应用广泛；3D高斯泼溅实现实时逼真新视角渲染，水下变体引入物理成像模型分离介质效应与场景辐射，但其重建质量受限于初始化所用几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下3D重建在海洋生态监测、水下检测、考古、教育与沉浸式可视化等方面具有应用价值。3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）使实时、逼真的新视角渲染成为可能，水下变体进一步结合基于物理的成像模型，将水体介质效应与场景辐射分离。然而，这类方法的重建质量从根本上受限于初始化所依赖的几何质量。

Method: 该摘要为问题陈述型文本，未给出具体技术方案。所述背景方法为：(1) 3D高斯泼溅，以高斯图元表示场景并实现实时新视角合成；(2) 水下3DGS变体，采用基于物理的图像形成模型（image-formation model）建模水体吸收、散射等介质效应，从而解耦介质效应与场景辐射（scene radiance）估计。摘要未披露初始化几何的具体来源与处理方式。

Result: 摘要未提供实验数据或定量结果，仅指出限制性结论：现有水下3DGS类方法的重建质量仍从根本上受限于用于初始化的几何（initialisation geometry）。

Conclusion: 水下3D重建结合实时渲染具有重要应用前景，物理成像模型有助于分离介质效应与场景辐射；但初始化几何是制约重建质量的根本瓶颈，提示后续研究需改进几何初始化策略以提升水下重建性能。

Abstract: Underwater 3D reconstruction supports applications ranging from marine ecosystem monitoring and subsea inspection to underwater archaeology, education, and immersive visualisation. 3D Gaussian Splatting has made real-time photorealistic novel-view rendering practical, while underwater variants incorporate physically based image-formation models to separate medium effects from scene radiance. Their reconstruction quality, however, remains fundamentally limited by the geometry used for initialisation.

</details>


### [82] [Toward Markerless Video-based Tremor Analysis: Objective Quantification of Pathological Tremor in Mouse Preclinical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18753)
*Yota Koshimoto,Akihiro Tsukahara,Yasuhiro Moriwaki,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅使用普通RGB相机的非侵入式小鼠震颤严重程度估计方法，通过分割预处理与震颤分数估计模块，在无约束小鼠实验中与加速度计测量高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 震颤是帕金森病和特发性震颤等神经系统疾病的标志性症状，其机制研究高度依赖小鼠模型。现有定量观测手段（肌电图、加速度计）存在成本高、装置复杂、需侵入性手术植入从而给动物带来显著应激等问题；而基于RGB的方法虽非侵入且低成本，却往往缺乏检测细微震颤所需的灵敏度。

Method: 提出仅用普通RGB相机的震颤严重程度估计流程：引入基于分割的预处理以提取小鼠区域，并设计震颤分数估计模块（Tremor Score Estimation Module），以高灵敏度捕捉细微震颤，从而在小鼠自身运动的同时分离出与震颤相关的振动信号。

Result: 在无约束小鼠实验中，使用两台标准相机以非侵入方式评估震颤。结果表明该方法与加速度计测量结果高度相关，并准确捕捉到震颤的强度依赖性特征。

Conclusion: 仅凭普通RGB相机即可实现非侵入、低成本且灵敏的小鼠震颤严重程度估计，为震颤病理生理研究提供了一种可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Tremor is a movement disorder characterized by involuntary, rhythmic oscillations of body parts and is a hallmark of several neurological conditions, including Parkinson's disease and essential tremor. Elucidating its underlying mechanisms relies heavily on mouse models, which offer genetic manipulability and translational relevance to human neural circuitry. Accordingly, these models are indispensable for studying tremor pathophysiology. So far, electromyography and accelerometers have been used as methods to quantitatively observe tremors in mice. However, these methods have several drawbacks, such as high costs and complex setups. In particular, the invasive surgical implantation of devices causes significant stress to the animals. Although RGB-based methods offer non-invasive and cost-effective alternatives, they often lack the sensitivity required to detect subtle tremors. Therefore, this paper addresses these challenges by achieving mouse tremor severity estimation using conventional RGB cameras only. To address the challenging task of isolating tremor-related vibrations while the mouse itself is also in motion, our pipeline incorporates segmentation-based pre-processing to extract the mouse region and a Tremor Score Estimation Module that captures subtle tremors with high sensitivity. In the experiments, we assessed tremors in unrestrained mice using a non-invasive method with two standard cameras. The results demonstrated a strong correlation with accelerometer measurements and confirmed that the method accurately captured the intensity-dependent characteristics of tremors. The project page is available at https://isogawalab.github.io/Video-based-Tremor-Analysis-Project/.

</details>


### [83] [DISTA-Net++: Rethinking Infrared Small Target Unmixing Beyond Sub-Pixel Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18773)
*Mengze Xu,Zhu Liu,Weidong Sheng,Boyang Li,Yimian Dai,Ming-Ming Cheng,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对远距离红外成像中密集目标簇难以分辨的问题，提出"有信息引导、连续化"的解混新范式，构建了首个开源生态（CSIST-100K基准、专用指标、GrokCSO工具包），并提出DISTA-Net++，通过计数引导先验与连续坐标校正实现高精度、低算力的亚像素解混。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离红外成像中，密集目标簇的衍射极限特征会融合为单一模糊斑块，掩盖目标数量、亚像素位置与辐射强度。现有深度学习通用目标检测难以解决"近距离红外小目标（CSIST）"问题，原因在于该领域存在系统性基础设施空白以及根本性范式错配：主流方法将解混视为盲目的离散亚像素分离，缺乏语义引导导致病态逆问题解具有歧义（虚警与漏检），且基于网格的离散化将预测锁定在固定格点，使精度依赖昂贵的高分辨率网格细化。

Method: 作者主张CSIST解混应"有信息引导且连续"，并构建首个完整开源生态：大规模基准CSIST-100K、专用指标套件与GrokCSO工具包。基于该基础提出DISTA-Net++，以动态深度展开网络为骨干，引入两个协同机制：计数引导先验（将全局目标数量作为显式语义约束以正则化解空间）与连续坐标校正（回归离网格偏移，使定位精度与网格分辨率解耦）。

Result: 大量实验验证了新范式：即使在最经济的3倍划分下，DISTA-Net++在CSO-mAP上超过7倍划分的最先进方法16.15%，计数准确率高出62.96%，而计算量仅为后者的六分之一，表明解混精度无需以更细的离散化换取。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了CSIST解混的"有信息引导、连续化"新范式，并证明通过语义约束与连续坐标回归可同时提升精度与效率。完整生态开源。

Abstract: Long-range infrared imaging frequently confronts dense target clusters whose diffraction-limited signatures merge into a single indistinguishable blob, concealing the number, sub-pixel positions, and radiant intensities of the underlying sources. While deep learning has advanced general object detection, resolving such Closely-Spaced Infrared Small Targets (CSIST) remains largely unexplored, owing to a systemic infrastructure void and a fundamental paradigm mismatch. The dominant formulation, which reduces unmixing to a blind, discrete sub-pixel separation, is inherently insufficient: without semantic guidance, the ill-posed inverse problem admits ambiguous solutions plagued by false and missed detections, while grid-based discretization locks predictions onto fixed lattice centers, chaining precision to prohibitively expensive grid refinement. We argue that CSIST unmixing should instead be informed and continuous. To ground this paradigm shift, we establish the first comprehensive open-source ecosystem for the field, comprising the large-scale CSIST-100K benchmark, a tailored metric suite, and the GrokCSO toolkit. Upon this foundation, we propose DISTA-Net++, which anchors a dynamic deep unfolding backbone with two synergistic mechanisms: a Count-Guided Prior that injects the global target count as an explicit semantic constraint to regularize the solution space, and a Continuous Coordinate Rectification that regresses off-grid offsets to decouple localization accuracy from grid resolution. Extensive experiments validate our paradigm: even under the most economical 3x division, DISTA-Net++ surpasses 7x-division state-of-the-art methods by 16.15% in CSO-mAP and 62.96% in count accuracy at merely one-sixth of their computation, demonstrating that unmixing precision need not be purchased with finer discretization. The complete ecosystem is available at https://github.com/GrokCV/GrokDet.

</details>


### [84] [Using OCR Heads to Verbalize Image Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18823)
*Sheridan Feucht,Benno Krojer,Sarah Wang,Henry Abrahamsen,Byron C. Wallace,David Bau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过研究VLM中的OCR机制，发现存在因果必要的通用语义注意力头，可提取跨层可解释语义特征，并支持概念编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VLM如何从像素映射到语义这一广义问题，本文聚焦OCR这一具体任务作为切入点，以揭示更广泛的机制。

Method: 在4个模型上识别对OCR因果必要的注意力头；将这些头的注意力权重压缩为单一"verbalization lens"变换，并结合投影到词表空间以获取可解释标签；利用该变换的逆进行非词汇概念编辑。

Result: 识别出的头实际上是通用语义头，能对所有图像token输出可解释语义特征；结合词表投影可从第0层起获取可解释标签，表明图像表征在早期层即与语言对齐；逆变换可替换自然图像中的概念（如拖拉机换为左轮手枪），证明该子空间具有超出OCR的因果作用。

Conclusion: 对特定机制的研究可揭示更广泛的VLM可解释性问题。

Abstract: How do VLMs map from pixels to semantics? To understand this general question, we focus on a narrow one: studying how VLMs perform optical character recognition (OCR). Across four models, we identify attention heads causally necessary for OCR, and discover that these are in fact general-purpose heads that output interpretable semantic features across all image tokens. For example, pointing these heads at an image token containing the word "bike" causes Qwen3-VL-8B to output "bike," but pointing them at a bird wing causes the model to output the token "feathers." We collapse these heads' attention weights into a single verbalization lens transformation that reveals interpretable semantic features in hidden states across all layers. When combined with projection to vocabulary space, we can obtain interpretable labels starting from layer 0, showing that image representations are in fact aligned with language in early layers. We find that we can also use the inverse of this transformation to edit non-word concepts, e.g., replacing a tractor with a revolver in a naturalistic image, providing causal evidence that this subspace is useful for more than just OCR. Our results are an example of how the study of specific mechanisms can shed light on broader interpretability problems.

</details>


### [85] [Copy What Is Seen, Generate What Is Not: Training-Free Anomaly-Aware Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18836)
*Zhida Qu,Shengchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AVR（Anomaly-aware Video Restoration），仅用冻结的预训练模型，将免训练视频异常检测与免训练视频修复打通：先由运动证据将开放词汇提案门控为时空掩码，再用背景先验填补异常暴露的像素，扩散模型只合成任何帧都未呈现的内容，并由冻结验证器逐片段选择修复器。在三个监控数据集上，AVR 在全参考注入与真实异常两种设定下均达全帧保真领先，编辑区域可媲美三个已训练视频修复器，并优于"先检测再生成"流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控视频中的异常检测与修复本应协同，但现有研究彼此孤立：免训练异常检测器只输出分数或标签，不修复画面；免训练视频编辑则响应用户提示而非检测器信号，因此无法自动、定向地修复被检测到的异常区域，形成"检测—修复"之间的空白。

Method: 方法完全基于冻结预训练模型：（1）用运动证据将开放词汇提案门控为时空掩码；（2）由片段计算背景先验，填充异常曾暴露的所有像素；（3）扩散模型仅负责合成无任何帧提供证据的内容；（4）由冻结验证器逐片段在经典修复器、先验锚定修复器、背景条件修复器之间做出选择。

Result: 在三个监控数据集、全参考异常注入与真实异常两种设定下进行大量实验：AVR 在 oracle 掩码下取得领先的全帧保真度；在编辑区域内可匹配三个已训练视频修复器；在自身生成的掩码上优于"先检测再生成"流程；同时抑制了残留异常与自由扩散带来的闪烁。

Conclusion: AVR 证明仅凭冻结预训练模型即可把异常检测与视频修复统一到同一免训练框架中，通过运动门控掩码、背景先验填充与冻结验证器路由的三段式设计，实现高保真、低闪烁的异常感知视频修复，为监控场景下的自动检测—修复一体化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A surveillance system that detects an anomaly often has to repair the footage as well, yet the two tasks are studied in isolation: training-free anomaly detectors stop at a score or a label, while training-free video editing answers to a user prompt rather than to a detector. This paper proposes AVR (Anomaly-aware Video Restoration), which closes that gap with frozen pretrained models alone and generates content only where the clip offers no evidence to copy. Motion evidence first gates open-vocabulary proposals into spatio-temporal masks. A background prior computed from the clip then fills every pixel the anomaly ever uncovers, leaving diffusion to synthesize only what no frame showed, and a frozen verifier decides per clip whether to trust a classical, a prior-anchored, or a background-conditioned restorer. Extensive experiments on three surveillance datasets, under both full-reference anomaly injection and real anomalies, show that AVR leads full-frame fidelity under oracle masks, matches three trained video inpainters inside the edited region, and outperforms a detect-then-generate pipeline on the masks it produces itself, while suppressing both the residual anomaly and the flicker of free diffusion.

</details>


### [86] [Decodable but Misrouted: Sparse Features Uncover a Readout Gap in Vision-Language Models for Harmful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18860)
*Girish A. Koushik,Diptesh Kanojia,Helen Treharne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文区分大型视觉语言模型在有害表情包分类失败中的两种成因——内部证据缺失与证据路由失败，并通过稀疏自编码器、角色条件探针、因果干预与恢复实验加以验证，发现"路由"而非"表征"是持续存在的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当大型视觉语言模型误判有害表情包时，失败可能源于内部证据缺失，也可能源于模型无法将已表征的证据路由到输出层。作者希望区分这两种失败模式，从而定位真正的瓶颈所在。

Method: 在 Gemma-3 与 Qwen3.5 上使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）、角色条件探针、因果干预与恢复实验，覆盖六个有害内容基准，并补充西班牙语及印地语-英语语码混合评测；开展特征消融与路由特征 patch 的敏感性对比、仅校准路由、探针蒸馏 LoRA 及 Gemma-3-12B 在 Facebook Hateful Memes 上的案例研究，并设置鲁棒性对照。

Result: 稀疏读出在六个二分类主任务上均优于原生预测（Qwen 宏 F1 均值 0.740 对原生 0.432，残差重建 0.486；Gemma 从 0.532 提升至 0.714）；Qwen 静默特征消融对探针敏感度高 24–63 倍，而字面 yes/no 任务的路由特征 patch 对输出敏感度高 16–140 倍；仅校准路由可恢复 93.3% 的平均差距，探针蒸馏 LoRA 改善原生预测但共享多任务适配出现负迁移；案例研究得到分布式 rank-32 图文交互，宏 F1 0.756 对原生 0.685。

Conclusion: 信号可跨语言泛化，不能仅由 OCR 解释，且依赖配对的视觉证据；路由（而非单纯的表征）是有害表情包分类中反复出现的瓶颈。

Abstract: When a large vision-language model misclassifies a harmful meme, the failure may reflect missing internal evidence or an inability to route represented evidence to its output. We distinguish these cases in Gemma-3 and Qwen3.5 using sparse autoencoders, role-conditioned probes, causal interventions, and recovery experiments across six harmful content benchmarks, with additional Spanish and Hindi-English code-mixed evaluations. Sparse readouts outperform native prediction on all six primary binary tasks: Qwen averages $0.740$ versus $0.432$ native macro-F1, while residual reconstruction reaches $0.486$, whereas Gemma improves from $0.532$ to $0.714$. These differences reflect supervised accessibility rather than a pre-existing, native decision rule, and the most influential token role depends on the task. Under the evaluated score scales, Qwen silent-feature ablation is $24-63$ times more probe-sensitive, whereas routed-feature patching on literal yes/no tasks is $16-140$ times more output-sensitive. Calibration-only routing recovers $93.3$% of the mean gap, and probe-distilled LoRA improves native predictions, although shared multi-task adaptation causes negative transfer. A case study of Gemma-3-12B on Facebook Hateful Memes finds a distributed rank-32 image-prompt interaction, reaching $0.756$ versus $0.685$ native macro-F1. Robustness controls show that the signal extends beyond English, is not explained solely by accompanying OCR, and depends on paired visual evidence. Thus, routing, rather than representation alone, is a recurring bottleneck in harmful meme classification.

</details>


### [87] [NormLift: From Lifted Features To Semantic Reliability In 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18898)
*Yihan Zang,Da Li,Dominik Engel,Shinkyu Park,Ivan Viola*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从3D侧重新审视2D语义特征到3D高斯的迁移问题，将逐高斯语义分配建模为CLIP单位球面上的余弦对齐问题，证明L2归一化的语义反投影特征是闭式解，并由此导出特征模长作为语义可靠性信号，提出免训练框架NormLift。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练加权聚合方法多从渲染角度解释，将高斯特征视为可线性组合的欧氏变量以重建2D特征图；但下游3D使用中，每个高斯通常在余弦嵌入空间中独立查询，两者不匹配，因此该操作的理论作用尚未被充分理解。

Method: 作者从3D侧出发，将逐高斯分配形式化为CLIP单位球面上的余弦对齐问题；在该目标下，L2归一化的语义反投影特征构成闭式解。该形式化进一步给出模长的分解，分为视图内一致性与视图间一致性，表明特征模长可作为语义可靠性信号；该可靠性分数经有效多视图支持校准后，引导一种模式投票（mode-voting）精化，通过避免线性平均来保持CLIP特征的有效性。

Result: 在开放词汇3D语义分割任务上，NormLift在各评估协议下均取得强劲性能，且为高效、免训练框架。

Conclusion: 标准特征提升规则可从逐高斯语义分配角度获得互补解释；特征模长可作为语义可靠性信号用于精化，避免线性平均带来的CLIP特征失真，验证了免训练加权聚合在3D侧的合理性。

Abstract: Training-free weighted aggregation is widely used to lift 2D semantic features onto 3D Gaussians for open-vocabulary scene understanding, yet its theoretical role remains insufficiently understood. Existing analyses typically justify this operation from the rendering side, treating Gaussian features as linearly composable Euclidean variables for reconstructing 2D feature maps. However, this view does not match downstream 3D usage, where each Gaussian is often queried independently in a cosine-based embedding space. We revisit feature lifting from the 3D side and formulate per-Gaussian assignment as a cosine alignment problem on the CLIP unit sphere. Under this objective, the L2-normalized semantic back-projected feature emerges as the closed-form solution, providing a complementary interpretation of the standard lifting rule from the perspective of per-Gaussian semantic assignment. The same formulation further yields a norm decomposition into intra-view and inter-view consistency, suggesting that feature magnitude itself can serve as a semantic reliability signal. Calibrated by effective multi-view support, this reliability score guides a mode-voting refinement that preserves CLIP feature validity by avoiding linear averaging. Experiments on open-vocabulary 3D semantic segmentation show that NormLift is an efficient, training-free framework that achieves strong performance across evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [88] [PhysVGGT: Feed-Forward Dense Physical Property Estimation from A Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18920)
*Sneha Paul,Guile Wu,Bingbing Liu,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysVGGT，一种前馈模型，从单张RGB图像一次性预测稠密摩擦系数、邵氏硬度、杨氏模量、密度图及物体级质量，推理仅0.13秒，比先前最优快27倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理属性（摩擦、硬度、刚度、密度）决定机器人如何抓取和操作物体，但从RGB图像估计这些属性仍具挑战。现有方法要么对每个物体做重建并附加物理属性，要么在测试时查询视觉语言模型，导致计算开销巨大，限制了实际应用。

Method: 将物理属性估计建模为逐像素稠密预测问题；采用视觉几何Transformer从输入图像提取几何感知token，随后接稠密预测分支估计局部物理属性、全局预测分支估计物体级质量。同时提出可扩展的伪标签生成流水线，实现大规模弱监督训练，减少对昂贵实测物理量的依赖。

Result: 在ABO-500数据集上达到最优性能，并有效泛化到分布外的NeRF2Physics数据集；无需逐物体重建与测试时优化，单图推理延迟仅0.13秒，比先前最优快27倍。

Conclusion: PhysVGGT通过前馈稠密预测范式，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低计算开销，为机器人操作中的实时物理属性估计提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Physical properties, such as friction, hardness, stiffness, and density, govern how robots should grasp, manipulate and interact with objects, yet estimating these properties from RGB images remains challenging. Existing methods typically employ per-object reconstruction augmented with physical properties or directly query vision-language models at test time, which results in substantial computational overhead that limits their applicability. In this work, we present PhysVGGT, a feed-forward model that predicts dense maps of friction coefficient, Shore hardness, Young's modulus, and density, together with object-level mass, from a single RGB image in one forward pass. The key idea of PhysVGGT is to formulate physical property estimation as a dense per-pixel prediction problem and employ a visual geometry transformer to extract geometry-aware tokens from the input image followed by a dense prediction branch for estimating local physical properties and a global prediction branch for estimating object-level mass. In addition, we introduce a scalable pseudo-label generation pipeline that enables large-scale weakly supervised training for dense physical property prediction, substantially reducing the need for expensive direct physical measurements. Extensive experiments show that PhysVGGT achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ABO-500 dataset and generalizes effectively to the out-of-distribution NeRF2Physics dataset. Moreover, PhysVGGT eliminates the need for per-object reconstruction and test-time optimization, achieving an inference latency of only 0.13s per image, making it $27\times$ faster than the previous state of the art.

</details>


### [89] [Dose-Aware Cold Diffusion with Physics Consistency for Generalizable Low-Dose CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18943)
*Md Imam Ahasan,Guangchao Yang,A F M Abdun Noor,S M Hasan Mahmud,Md Mahfuzur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出剂量感知冷扩散（DACD）框架，将辐射剂量建模为连续隐变量，实现跨连续及未见剂量水平的低剂量CT稳健重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低CT辐射剂量会显著降低图像质量，影响重建的准确性与临床可靠性。现有低剂量CT方法难以泛化到连续且训练中未见的剂量水平，易产生伪影并丢失解剖细节。

Method: DACD在冷扩散过程中显式建模辐射剂量的连续隐因子，集成基于图像的剂量感知、多尺度结构先验提取与剂量校准的步长分配以自适应引导去噪轨迹；并在反向细化中引入迭代前向-反投影校正，以强化投影域数据一致性。

Result: 在Mayo-2020、Mayo-2016和LoDoPaB-CT三个公开基准上，DACD在定量准确性与视觉保真度上均优于当前最优的扩散类和物理引导方法，尤其在超低剂量条件下优势明显，并对包括训练中未见剂量在内的连续剂量范围保持稳健泛化。

Conclusion: 将剂量作为连续隐因子嵌入冷扩散并引入投影域一致性校正，可有效提升低剂量CT重建的泛化能力与重建质量。

Abstract: Reducing radiation dose in computed tomography significantly degrades image quality and poses challenges for accurate and clinically reliable reconstruction. While recent approaches have shown promise for low-dose CT, they often struggle to generalize across continuous and previously unseen dose levels, leading to artifacts and loss of anatomical detail. To address these limitations, we propose Dose-Aware Cold Diffusion (DACD), a physics-consistent reconstruction framework that explicitly models radiation dose as a continuous latent factor within a cold diffusion process. The proposed DACD framework integrates image-based dose-aware perception, multi-scale structural prior extraction, and dose-calibrated step allocation to adaptively guide the denoising trajectory. In addition, an iterative forward-backprojection correction is incorporated into the reverse refinement process to enforce projection-domain data consistency. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks, including Mayo-2020, Mayo-2016, and LoDoPaB-CT, demonstrate that DACD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art diffusion-based and physics-guided methods in both quantitative accuracy and visual fidelity, particularly under ultra-low-dose conditions. The results show that DACD achieves robust generalization across a continuous range of dose levels, including those unseen during training.

</details>


### [90] [Automated Dental Caries Segmentation in Panoramic Radiographs Using Dual-Stage Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18952)
*Jihun Kim,Kyeonghun Kim,Jong-yeol Lee,Yeongseok Seo,Dohyun Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双阶段深度学习框架（Faster R-CNN定位牙齿 + U-Net像素级分割龋齿），并将多边形标注批量转换为高分辨率二值掩膜以突破标注瓶颈。在3000张全景片上训练，实现IoU 0.9013、Dice 0.9482、Recall 0.9433、Precision 0.9774，优于现有方法并显著降低假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统龋齿诊断方法有限，尤其对后牙邻面病损难以早期发现；深度学习虽有助于自动化检测，但依赖大量专家标注数据，可扩展性受限。

Method: 构建双阶段框架：第一阶段用Faster R-CNN进行牙齿定位，第二阶段用U-Net做像素级龋齿分割；设计系统性转换流水线，将大规模多边形标注数据集转换为高分辨率二值分割掩膜，从而支持像素级监督学习；训练数据结合专家核验数据集与经算法处理的3000张全景片标签。

Result: 取得稳定性能：IoU 0.9013，Dice系数0.9482，召回率0.9433，精确率0.9774；相比现有方法精度更优，且假阳性率显著降低。

Conclusion: 该双阶段框架有效缓解牙科AI应用中的数据标注瓶颈，展示出构建可扩展、自动化龋齿检测系统的潜力，可提升诊断一致性并支持临床决策。

Abstract: Early detection of dental caries remains challenging due to limitations in traditional diagnostic methods, particularly for proximal lesions in posterior teeth. Deep learning models show promise for automated caries detection but face scalability constraints due to requirements for large volumes of expertly annotated training data. This study presents a dual-stage deep learning framework combining Faster R-CNN for tooth localization with U-Net for pixel-wise caries segmentation in panoramic radiographs. We developed a systematic transformation pipeline to convert large-scale polygon-annotated datasets into high-resolution binary segmentation masks, enabling pixel-wise supervised learning. The framework was trained using both expert-verified datasets and algorithmically processed labels from 3,000 panoramic images. Our approach achieved robust performance with an IoU of 0.9013, Dice coefficient of 0.9482, Recall of 0.9433, and Precision of 0.9774, demonstrating superior accuracy compared to existing methods while significantly reducing false-positive rates. The dual-stage framework effectively addresses data annotation bottlenecks in dental AI applications and demonstrates potential for scalable, automated caries detection systems that can improve diagnostic consistency and support clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [91] [KDTwin: Task-Aware Knowledge Distillation for Lightweight Multi-Task Driving Scene Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18955)
*Huy Che,Minh-Khoi Do,Dinh-Duy Phan,Duc-Khai Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出任务感知蒸馏框架KDTwin，针对轻量多任务驾驶场景分割，在不增加推理开销的前提下提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时自动驾驶需要高效感知模型，需平衡精度与计算成本。但将知识蒸馏用于多任务驾驶场景分割存在困难：可行驶区域分割与车道线分割具有不同的空间特性，且类别不平衡问题各异，难以用统一的蒸馏目标处理。

Method: KDTwin在共享编码器和任务特定解码器两个层面进行蒸馏。编码器层面采用成对蒸馏（pairwise distillation），传递空间关系知识以增强学生网络的共享表示。解码器层面针对任务定制损失：可行驶区域分割使用加权损失，车道线分割使用边界感知损失，从而实现任务自适应知识迁移，且不增加推理复杂度。

Result: 在BDD100K数据集上，对多种基于CNN和基于Transformer的学生模型均取得一致性提升，且未增加推理阶段的参数量与FLOPs。

Conclusion: 结果表明，根据任务特定特性设计蒸馏目标，可有效提升自动驾驶多任务分割性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Efficient perception models are essential for real-time autonomous driving, where accuracy and computational cost must be carefully balanced. However, applying knowledge distillation to multi-task driving scene segmentation is challenging because drivable-area and lane segmentation exhibit different spatial characteristics and class imbalance. We propose KDTwin, a task-aware distillation framework for lightweight multi-task segmentation networks. The proposed method performs distillation at both the shared encoder and task-specific decoders. Encoder-level pairwise distillation transfers spatial relational knowledge to enhance the student's shared representation. For the decoders, we use a weighted loss for drivable-area segmentation and a boundary-aware loss for lane segmentation, enabling task-adaptive knowledge transfer without increasing inference complexity. Experiments on BDD100K show consistent improvements across the evaluated CNN-based and Transformer-based student models without increasing inference-time parameters or FLOPs. The results show that designing distillation objectives according to task-specific characteristics can effectively enhance multi-task segmentation performance for autonomous driving. The source code is available at https://github.com/chequanghuy/KDTwin.

</details>


### [92] [LaSeD: Label-Semantic Self-Distillation for Visual-Only Surgical Phase Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18971)
*Ye Tao,Claudia Scherl,Sara Monji-Azad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LaSeD标签语义自蒸馏框架，将手术阶段名称作为特权训练上下文，使视觉语言模型在仅用图像推理时仍能获得阶段名提示的增益，在Cholec80上显著超越纯视觉基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术阶段识别需要将视频帧映射到临床工作流阶段。现有方法大多仅把阶段标注当作类别ID，而近期手术视觉语言模型往往依赖额外的视频-文本数据、描述文本或指令微调，成本较高。作者希望以低成本方式利用阶段名称这一语义信息来适配VLM。

Method: 提出LaSeD——一种标签语义自蒸馏框架。从同一预训练VLM检查点初始化冻结的教师模型和学生模型。教师接收帧、固定任务提示与真实阶段名提示；学生仅接收帧和提示、不含阶段名提示，且只优化其视觉编码器。训练结合硬阶段token监督与来自缓存教师表示的特征级蒸馏。推理时移除教师和提示，学生通过受限数字token logits直接预测Cholec80的七个阶段，无需额外分类头。

Result: 在Cholec80评测划分上，LaSeD取得86.20%准确率、77.75%宏召回、78.13%宏精确率和64.15%宏Jaccard。在相同协议下，相较纯视觉Qwen3-VL-4B基线分别提升9.45、9.42、11.93和12.22个百分点。纯视觉指图像是唯一的样本特定推理输入，所有帧共享同一固定任务提示。

Conclusion: 结果表明阶段名称是一种有用且低成本的信号，可用于将视觉语言模型适配到手术工作流分析，同时保持纯视觉部署能力。代码将很快公开。

Abstract: Surgical phase recognition maps each video frame to a clinically meaningful workflow phase, supporting context-aware assistance, documentation, and postoperative analysis. Most methods treat phase annotations only as class IDs, whereas recent surgical vision-language models often require additional video--text data, captions, or instruction tuning. We propose \emph{LaSeD}, a label-semantic self-distillation framework that uses phase names as privileged training-time context while retaining visual-only deployment without a ground-truth phase-name hint. LaSeD initializes a frozen teacher and a student from the same pretrained VLM checkpoint. The teacher receives the frame, a fixed task prompt, and the ground-truth phase-name hint; the student receives the same frame and prompt without the hint, and only its visual encoder is optimized. Training combines hard phase-token supervision with feature-level distillation from cached teacher representations. At inference, the teacher and hint are removed, and the student predicts one of the seven Cholec80 phases through constrained digit-token logits without an additional classifier head. On the Cholec80 evaluation split, LaSeD achieves 86.20\% accuracy, 77.75\% macro recall, 78.13\% macro precision, and 64.15\% macro Jaccard. Under the identical protocol, it improves a visual-only Qwen3-VL-4B baseline by 9.45, 9.42, 11.93, and 12.22 percentage points, respectively. Visual-only means that the image is the only sample-specific inference input, while all frames share the same fixed task prompt. These results suggest that phase names provide a useful low-cost signal for adapting VLMs to surgical workflow analysis. (The code will be published soon.)

</details>


### [93] [Instrument Classification of Solo Sheet Music Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18980)
*Kevin Ji,Daniel Yang,TJ Tsai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将乐谱图像转为基于bootleg score的"音乐词"序列，把乐器分类当作文本分类任务，通过无监督语言模型预训练+微调，并引入两种数据增强方法，显著提升了单乐器乐谱图像分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往乐器识别研究多集中于音频数据，而本文关注原始乐谱图像上的乐器分类问题，探索将乐谱转换为文本序列后进行语言模型建模的可行性。

Method: 先将乐谱图像转换为基于bootleg score表示的"音乐词"序列，把乐器分类视为文本分类任务。使用IMSLP的单乐器乐谱图像（涵盖八种乐器）训练AWD-LSTM、GPT-2和RoBERTa模型，在无标注数据上预训练语言模型，用预训练权重初始化分类器，再在标注数据上微调；同时提出两种数据增强方法。

Result: GPT-2和RoBERTa略优于AWD-LSTM；预训练使RoBERTa的分类准确率从34.5%提升至42.9%；两种数据增强方法在此基础上再提升约15%。

Conclusion: 将乐谱图像转化为文本序列并借助语言模型预训练与微调，可有效提升乐器分类准确率，数据增强进一步带来增益，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies instrument classification of solo sheet music. Whereas previous work has focused on instrument recognition in audio data, we instead approach the instrument classification problem using raw sheet music images. Our approach first converts the sheet music image into a sequence of musical "words" based on the bootleg score representation, and then treats the problem as a text classification task. We show that it is possible to significantly improve classifier performance by training a language model on unlabeled data, initializing a classifier with the pretrained language model weights, and then finetuning the classifier on labeled data. In this work, we train AWD-LSTM, GPT-2, and RoBERTa models on solo sheet music images from IMSLP for eight different instruments. We find that GPT-2 and RoBERTa slightly outperform AWD-LSTM, and that pretraining increases classification accuracy for RoBERTa from 34.5% to 42.9%. Furthermore, we propose two data augmentation methods that increase classification accuracy for RoBERTa by an additional 15%.

</details>


### [94] [Track, Articulate, Act: Generating Articulation from Casual Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19119)
*Jiaming Zhang,Homanga Bharadhwaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出real-to-sim框架，从单目RGB视频重建可用于仿真的关节物体及手-物交互，无需深度、多视图、先验扫描、人工关节标注或机器人示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频蕴含丰富的因果证据，揭示手部运动如何引发物体运动并产生任务相关的状态变化，可为机器人操作提供学习信号。门、抽屉、橱柜、笔记本电脑、烤箱等关节物体在日常生活中无处不在，但其运动依赖内部部件与关节，无法用单一位姿表示，且数据获取通常依赖RGB-D、多视角或先验标注，构成具身交互的独特挑战。作者希望仅凭随手拍摄的单目RGB视频即可获得可仿真的关节物体模型。

Method: 核心洞察是稠密3D点轨迹提供了与具体具身无关的关节线索：固定连杆上的点近似静止，运动连杆上的点呈一致的旋转或平移运动。方法将连杆分割、估计关节及其状态轨迹、重建关节资产，并将恢复的3D手部运动与物体对齐。实现上采用模块化方案，复用强大的预训练模型进行单图3D重建、网格分割与3D场景流，通过显式几何推理将各模型预测串联起来以推断关节结构。最后利用重建的关节物体与手部轨迹，在MuJoCo中通过接触回放交互。

Result: 在无需RGB-D、多视图输入、先验扫描、人工指定关节或机器人示范的条件下，从随意的单目RGB视频成功重建出仿真就绪的关节物体与手-物交互，并能在MuJoCo中通过接触回放该交互。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，预训练视觉模型结合显式的运动推理，能够将随意的人类视频转化为适用于下游具身交互的关节物体模型，为real-to-sim的关节物体建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Human videos contain rich causal evidence for robot manipulation: they reveal how hand motion induces object motion and produces task-relevant changes in object state. In this work, we study articulated objects such as doors, drawers, cabinets, laptops, ovens, and hinged containers that are ubiquitous in daily life and present unique challenges for embodied interaction. These objects cannot be represented by a single pose; their motion depends on the underlying parts and joints. We introduce a real-to-sim framework that reconstructs a simulation-ready articulated object and hand-object interaction from a casual monocular RGB video, without RGB-D or multi-view input, prior scans, manually specified joints, or robot demonstrations. Our key insight is that dense 3D point tracks provide an embodiment-agnostic articulation cue: points on the fixed link remain approximately stationary, while points on the moving link follow coherent revolute or prismatic motion. Our method segments the links, estimates the joint and its state trajectory, reconstructs an articulated asset, and aligns the recovered 3D hand motion with the object. Central to our approach is a modular recipe that repurposes powerful pretrained models for single-image 3D reconstruction, mesh segmentation, and 3D scene flow, connecting their predictions through explicit geometric reasoning to infer articulation. We use the reconstructed articulated object and the human hand trajectory to replay interactions through contact in MuJoCo. The framework shows how pretrained vision models and explicit motion reasoning can turn casual human videos into articulated object models suitable for downstream embodied interactions. https://track-articulate-act.github.io/

</details>


### [95] [Adaptive Convolutional Sparse Coding via Information Bottleneck for Robust Visual Signal Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19122)
*Meng'en Qin,Yinchen Liu,Mingxuan Cui,Youlu Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应卷积稀疏编码框架，将稀疏系数作为可微变量与网络参数联合学习，并从信息瓶颈视角加以解释，显著提升视觉信号表示的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉信号需要紧凑且充分的表示以实现鲁棒的下游预测。卷积稀疏编码（CSC）能显式抑制冗余成分并保留信号内容，但其稀疏系数通常固定且需人工选取，缺乏自适应性与鲁棒性。

Method: 用FISTA展开CSC优化过程，将稀疏系数视为可微变量并与网络参数联合学习；从信息瓶颈角度解释该系数控制信息保留与压缩的权衡（稀疏项促进紧凑表示，重构项与任务损失保留任务相关内容）；并引入无标签的训练后策略，在固定主网络参数下为受损输入调整压缩强度。

Result: 在CIFAR和ImageNet上的实验表明，该方法在干净数据上取得有竞争力的识别性能，并在不同输入扰动下大幅提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应学习稀疏系数可有效平衡表示的紧凑性与充分性，结合无标签后训练策略，能在保持干净数据性能的同时显著增强视觉表示的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Visual signals require compact yet sufficient representations for robust downstream prediction. Convolutional sparse coding (CSC) provides an explicit mechanism for suppressing redundant components while preserving signal content, but its sparsity coefficient is typically fixed and manually selected. We propose an adaptive convolutional sparse coding framework for robust visual signal representation. Specifically, we unfold the CSC optimization with the Fast Iterative Shrinkage-Thresholding Algorithm (FISTA) and treat the sparsity coefficient as a differentiable variable jointly learned with the network parameters. From the information bottleneck perspective, this coefficient controls the trade-off between information retention and compression: the sparsity term promotes compact representations, while the reconstruction term together with task loss preserves task-relevant signal content. We further introduce a label-free post-training strategy that adjusts the compression strength for corrupted inputs with the main network parameters fixed. Experiments on CIFAR and ImageNet demonstrate competitive clean-data recognition and greatly improved robustness under different input perturbations.

</details>


### [96] [In-Context Robot Learning with VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19138)
*Dongzhou Cheng,Taoran Yi,Ye Fang,Xingwu Zhang,Fan Feng,Yixuan Li,Gengxiong Zhuang,Rongze Wang,Shuai Yang,Wei Song,Weizhi Xue,Minyan Wu,Jie Gui,Jiaqi Wang,Tong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GPT-Policy 通用智能体框架，让商业视觉语言模型（VLM）在无梯度更新的情况下，通过上下文学习（ICL）从人类视频演示、动作示例和交互反馈中学习，并转化为可执行、可验证的机器人行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让机器人像人类一样快速适应陌生环境是具身智能的"登月"目标。有限数量的演示无法覆盖机器人遇到的所有任务与情境，因此部署时从上下文中学习的能力对泛化至关重要。然而现有机器人策略基本无法实现这种上下文学习（ICL）。商业 VLM 展现出广泛的智能体能力，引出一个关键问题：这类模型能否在无梯度更新、不持久改变任务特定参数的前提下，从演示、示例和交互反馈中学习，并迁移为从新初始状态出发的可执行、可验证行为。

Method: 提出 GPT-Policy——面向上下文机器人学习的通用智能体框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 上下文编译器，用于保留与任务相关的视觉状态转移；(2) VLM，负责提出机器人工具动作；(3) 受约束控制器，对每个动作进行验证与执行并反馈结果。评估方式包括任务成功率与效率指标、跨模型的匹配对比，以及受控的上下文消融实验。

Result: 在真实机器人试验中，人类视频演示即使不含机器人动作标签也能提升任务完成率；在对接触敏感的任务上，对齐的动作参考带来进一步增益。跨模型匹配对比与上下文消融实验揭示了该框架的可靠性与局限。

Conclusion: GPT-Policy 是迈向通过上下文学习实现机器人适应的一步，为将 VLM 的通用能力转化为物理行为提供了实证基础，并明确了实现可靠部署仍需克服的挑战。

Abstract: Enabling robots to adapt to unfamiliar environments as readily as humans remains a moonshot goal of embodied AI. No finite collection of demonstrations can cover every task and situation a robot will encounter, making the ability to learn from context at deployment essential for generalization. Such in-context learning (ICL), however, remains largely beyond the reach of existing robotic policies. The broad agentic capabilities of commercial vision-language models (VLMs), such as GPT-6 Astra, raise a compelling question: can these models learn from demonstrations, examples, and interaction feedback, then translate that information into executable and verifiable robot behavior from a new initial state without gradient updates or persistent changes to task-specific parameters? We introduce GPT-Policy, a general-agent framework for in-context robot learning. GPT-Policy integrates a context compiler that preserves task-relevant visual transitions, a VLM that proposes robot-tool actions, and a constrained controller that verifies and executes each action and reports its outcome. We evaluate its reliability and limitations through task success and efficiency metrics, matched comparisons across models, and controlled context ablations. In real-robot trials, human video demonstrations improve task completion even without robot action labels, while aligned action references yield further gains on contact-sensitive tasks. These findings position GPT-Policy as a step toward robot adaptation through in-context learning, providing an empirical foundation for translating the general-purpose capabilities of VLMs into physical behavior and clarifying the challenges that must be overcome for reliable deployment.

</details>


### [97] [PointZero: 3D Point Track Completion for Learning Transferable 3D Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19142)
*Bardienus P. Duisterhof,Kaifeng Zhang,Adam Hung,Bowen Wen,Stan Birchfield,Yunzhu Li,Deva Ramanan,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以3D点轨迹补全作为预训练目标，无需机器人动作标签即可学习可迁移的3D动力学先验，并发布包含290万合成帧的多样化数据集，训练出PointZero模型，在动作条件3D动力学预测和模仿学习两个下游任务上超越或持平基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需在多样化数据上训练以形成丰富先验，但现有方法通常依赖机器人动作标签来学习动作条件3D动力学，导致网络视频等无标签数据无法纳入训练池。作者希望无需机器人数据即可学习可迁移的3D动力学。

Method: 将3D点轨迹补全作为预训练目标：给定单帧RGB-D观测和稀疏部分3D轨迹，预测所有已观测点的未来3D轨迹。构建涵盖可变形、铰接与刚性物体的290万合成帧数据集，训练基于Transformer的表达性模型PointZero，并可在下游微调用于动作条件3D动力学预测与模仿学习。

Result: PointZero在同一数据上优于先前方法；微调后以末端执行器位姿为条件，在PGND 3D动力学基准上超过基线；微调预测机器人动作与3D轨迹后，在7个仿真与真实操作任务中的6个上超越或持平基线。并从头训练以分离架构与预训练目标/数据集的贡献。

Conclusion: 3D点轨迹补全是一种有效且无需机器人动作标签的预训练目标，可产生丰富的3D动力学先验并迁移到多种下游机器人任务；作者公开数据集、检查点与完整训练方案。

Abstract: World models endow perceptual systems with the ability to predict how scenes evolve under interaction. They are most beneficial when trained on diverse volumes of data, to instill a rich prior into downstream applications. Existing methods typically require robot action labels to learn action-conditioned 3D dynamics, which excludes web video data from the training pool. We study 3D point track completion as a pre-training objective for learning transferable 3D dynamics without robot data. Given a single RGB-D observation and sparse partial 3D trajectories (tracks), we predict future 3D tracks of all observed points. We show this objective produces a rich 3D dynamics prior, without requiring robot action labels. We contribute a diverse dataset of 2.9 million synthetic frames spanning deformable, articulated, and rigid objects, and use it to train PointZero. We show that a flexible and expressive transformer, PointZero, outperforms prior methods on the same data. We demonstrate the utility of our pre-training objective by post-training PointZero for two downstream applications: (1) action-conditioned 3D dynamics prediction and (2) imitation learning. When fine-tuned to condition on end-effector pose, PointZero outperforms the baselines on the recent PGND 3D dynamics benchmark. When fine-tuned to predict robot actions and 3D tracks, PointZero outperforms or matches the baselines on 6/7 simulated and real-world robot manipulation tasks. We furthermore evaluate training PointZero from scratch to isolate the benefits of our proposed architecture from those of our proposed pre-training objective and dataset. We release the dataset, checkpoints, and full training recipe.

</details>


### [98] [PANORAMA: Panoptic Grounded Captioning via Mask Proposal Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19143)
*Sara Pieri,Evangelos Kazakos,Shizhe Chen,Josef Sivic,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出全景接地描述任务PanoCaps基准与PANORAMA模型，通过短语条件下的掩码选择实现精确的像素级接地与一致的详细描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型能生成流畅详细的图像描述，但难以可靠地将描述与像素关联；已有结合密集描述与像素级接地的方法往往描述不完整或分割掩码不准确。因此需要一个能同时覆盖前景与背景、且接地精准的综合图像理解框架。

Method: 1) 提出PanoCaps，一个基于全景分割数据集构建的人工标注基准，提供近乎完整像素覆盖的密集描述及实体级图文对齐，并设计短语-掩码匹配协议与广义全景质量（gPQ）指标。2) 将短语接地形式化为从短语条件下的掩码候选池中进行选择，提出PANORAMA：以语境化短语表示对预训练分割器进行条件化以生成候选掩码，并学习为每个短语选择对应掩码。3) 该接口与描述生成联合训练，支持短语对应单一区域或多实例。

Result: PANORAMA在PanoCaps上取得最佳整体接地效果，并在多个像素级接地任务上达到或超过专用模型；实验表明其能产生精确的实体级分割，同时保持详细且与掩码一致的描述。

Conclusion: 面向全景接地描述，短语条件下的掩码选择与描述联合训练可同时提升描述完整性与像素级接地的精度，为综合且空间接地的图像理解提供了有效方案，代码、数据与模型已公开。

Abstract: Intelligent systems that act in the world require image understanding that is both comprehensive and spatially grounded. Current vision-language models (VLMs) can generate fluent and detailed image captions, but reliably associating them with image pixels remains challenging. Existing methods that combine dense captioning with pixel-level grounding often produce either incomplete descriptions or inaccurate segmentation masks. We study this problem through panoptic grounded captioning, a task that requires a VLM to describe both foreground objects and background regions while grounding each referring phrase with pixel-level masks. We make three contributions. First, we introduce PanoCaps, a human-annotated benchmark constructed from panoptic segmentation datasets. It provides dense captions with near-complete pixel coverage and image-text alignments at the entity level, supporting both training and evaluation. We further propose a phrase-mask matching protocol and a generalized Panoptic Quality (gPQ) metric that jointly evaluates textual and mask agreement. Second, we formulate phrase grounding as selection from a phrase-conditioned pool of mask proposals and introduce PANORAMA, a VLM that conditions a pretrained segmenter on contextualized phrase representations to obtain candidate masks and learns to select those corresponding to each phrase. Training this interface jointly with caption generation enables PANORAMA to produce high-quality masks while allowing each phrase to refer to a single region or multiple instances. Third, PANORAMA achieves the best overall grounding on PanoCaps and matches or exceeds specialized models across several pixel-level grounding tasks. Experiments show that our method produces precise entity-level segmentations while maintaining detailed, mask-consistent captions. Code, data and models are available at https://www.di.ens.fr/willow/research/panorama/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Enhancing Extubation Failure Prediction with LLM-Derived Features from Respiratory Therapy Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17532)
*Izzy Chaiken,Aditya Khowal,Neha A. Sathe,Mark M. Wurfel,Lucy Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型从自由文本呼吸治疗记录中提取特征、并结合逻辑回归进行拔管失败（EF）预测的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有创机械通气是救命疗法，但需及时、安全地撤机以避免拔管失败及其健康风险，因此准确预测EF具有重要临床意义。

Method: 采用大语言模型对自由文本呼吸治疗记录中的特征进行分类提取，并结合逻辑回归构建预测流程；将该方法应用于华盛顿大学医学中心的患者队列，并把提取的特征与结构化患者数据结合进行EF预测。

Result: 方法识别出具有临床意义的EF相关特征，且这些特征与结构化数据联合使用时提升了EF预测性能；同时发现既往EF预测研究因目标人群差异（如纳入标准异质、EF定义不同）会导致模型性能的系统性差异，影响研究间的可推广性。

Conclusion: 利用大语言模型从自由文本呼吸治疗记录中提取特征可有效增强EF预测，且未来研究需关注目标人群与EF定义的一致性以提升可推广性。

Abstract: Invasive mechanical ventilation is a lifesaving therapy, but timely, safe discontinuation is essential to preventing extubation failure (EF) and related risks to health. We present a novel approach to EF prediction that leverages features classified in free-text respiratory therapy notes using a large language model and logistic regression pipeline. Applied to a patient cohort from University of Washington Medicine, our method identifies clinically meaningful EF-related features that improve EF prediction performance when included alongside structured patient data. We further highlight how differences in target populations in prior EF prediction studies, such as heterogenous inclusion criteria and EF definition, can lead to systematic differences in model performance and hinder generalizability between studies.

</details>


### [100] [Faking Good and Faking Bad in LLMs: Response Distortion Across Dark Triad Personality Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17534)
*Victoria Popa,Guglielmo Cola,Caterina Senette,Maurizio Tesconi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究考察7个当代大语言模型在"装好"(fake-good)与"装坏"(fake-bad)条件下对暗黑三联征(马基雅维利主义、自恋、精神病态)的表达调制,发现模型存在系统性的、随情境一致的作答扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会赞许性与印象管理是人类人格测评中普遍存在的作答扭曲来源,但其对大语言模型的影响研究不足。作者希望探究LLM是否会在社会赞许或非赞许激励下系统性地调节暗黑三联征特质的表达。

Method: 评估7个最先进模型,设置两种生态效度较高的情境:雇佣选拔与司法评估,通过情境框架传递社会赞许或非赞许激励。采用标准心理测量计分程序测量特质表达,并与自我评估基线在总体和题目层面进行比较;另设实验对比显性fake-bad指令与仅情境框架的效果差异。

Result: 模型表现出系统且随条件一致的作答调制:多数模型在fake-good条件下降低暗黑三联征得分,在fake-bad条件下提高得分,但效应大小与一致性因特质和模型而异。马基雅维利主义与自恋的转变最强且最一致,精神病态异质性较大。情境亦有影响,雇佣情境效应普遍大于司法情境。显性fake-bad指令产生的扭曲显著强于单纯情境框架。

Conclusion: 人格相关输出应结合其被诱发时的动机与情境背景加以解释。心理测量范式对于评估作答扭曲易感性、印象管理与情境依赖的行为转变具有价值,对LLM基准测试、对齐评估与鲁棒性评估有重要启示。

Abstract: Social desirability and impression management are pervasive sources of response distortion in human personality assessment, yet their effects on Large Language Models (LLMs) remain underexplored. This study investigates whether contemporary LLMs systematically modulate the expression of Dark Triad traits (Machiavellianism, narcissism, and psychopathy) under fake-good and fake-bad conditions. Seven state-of-the-art models were evaluated across two ecologically relevant contexts: employment selection and forensic evaluation, in which socially desirable or undesirable incentives were conveyed through contextual framing. Trait expression was measured using standard psychometric scoring procedures and compared with self-assessment baselines at both aggregate and item levels. Results revealed systematic and condition-consistent response modulation. Most models reduced Dark Triad scores under fake-good conditions and increased them under fake-bad conditions, although the magnitude and consistency of these effects varied across traits and models. Machiavellianism and narcissism showed the strongest and most coherent shifts, whereas psychopathy displayed greater heterogeneity. Context also influenced responses, with employment scenarios generally producing larger effects than forensic scenarios. An additional experiment showed that explicit fake-bad instructions generated substantially stronger distortions than contextual framing alone. The results suggest that personality-related outputs should be interpreted in light of the motivational and situational context in which they are elicited. More broadly, they highlight the value of psychometric paradigms for evaluating susceptibility to response distortion, impression management, and context-dependent behavioral shifts, with important implications for LLM benchmarking, alignment evaluation, and robustness assessment.

</details>


### [101] [DANTINOX: A Unified Framework for Multi-Paradigm Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17535)
*Marco Simoni,Aleksandar Fontana,Giulio Rossolini,Andrea Saracino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DantinoX 是一个开源 JAX/Flax 库，用单一模块化 Transformer 主干统一支持自回归解码、离散掩码扩散和连续流匹配三种语言生成范式，仅需改配置即可切换范式、注意力机制或硬件拓扑，从而实现受控的跨范式对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言生成研究目前涵盖三类范式：自回归解码、离散掩码扩散与连续流匹配。由于每种范式各自存在于独立代码库中，跨范式比较十分困难，所测得的差异往往反映的是实现细节而非范式本身的差异。因此需要一个统一平台以实现公平、受控的对比。

Method: 提出 DantinoX：一个开源 JAX/Flax 库。其核心是让同一模块化 Transformer 主干服务全部三种范式；切换生成范式、注意力机制或硬件拓扑只需更改配置，而主干架构、分词器、初始化策略与训练基础设施保持一致。通过统一 API 支持训练、流式推理与基准测试。

Result: 实现了在单一 API 内的受控跨范式比较，支持训练、流式推理与基准测试，并可通过配置变更灵活切换生成范式、注意力机制与硬件拓扑。

Conclusion: DantinoX 通过共享主干与统一基础设施，消除了因代码库差异引入的实现偏差，为三类语言生成范式的公平、可复现对比提供了开源工具基础。

Abstract: Language generation research increasingly spans three paradigms: autoregressive decoding, discrete masked diffusion, and continuous flow-matching. Comparing them is difficult because each lives in a separate codebase, so measured differences often reflect implementation details rather than the paradigms themselves. We present DantinoX, an open-source JAX/Flax library in which a single modular Transformer backbone serves all three paradigms. Switching the generation paradigm, attention mechanism, or hardware topology requires only a configuration change, while the backbone architecture, tokenizer, initialization strategy, and training infrastructure remain consistent. This enables controlled cross-paradigm comparisons within one API for training, streaming inference, and benchmarking.

</details>


### [102] [Think Before You Comfort: Reflective Cognitive Alignment for Protocol-Grounded Elderly Stimulation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17536)
*Jiyue Jiang,Ziyi Li,He Hu,Sheng Wang,Yuhan Chen,Yanyu Chen,Jingqi Zhou,Pengan Chen,Fei Ma,Irwin King,Yu Li,Chuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对认知刺激疗法(CST)难以规模化及低资源语言(如粤语)数据稀缺的问题，提出STaR-CS数据合成框架与RCA对齐框架，在六个大模型和两个独立评审上验证了协议遵循度、安全性与群体引导能力的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知刺激疗法(CST)为认知障碍老人提供非药物支持，但其规模化受限于对受训引导者的依赖，以及数据严重稀缺——对隐私敏感、低资源语言(如粤语)尤为突出。大语言模型虽在自动化陪伴上有潜力，却难以在共情互动与认知刺激指南遵循之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出双轴框架：其一为STaR-CS（风格迁移与角色条件化的认知刺激），通过引导者风格建模与结构化骨架提取合成多方对话，缓解数据瓶颈；其二为在此基础上构建的RCA（反思式认知对齐）框架，将刺激交互建模为序列决策过程，集成协议约束的认知链(PC-CoC)用于结构化推理，以及推理时价值对齐(IVA)用于基于安全与参与目标的原则化响应选择。

Result: 在六个骨干大语言模型和两个独立评审上的评估表明，RCA在协议遵循度、安全性和群体引导方面均稳定优于标准提示基线。

Conclusion: 所提框架在数据合成与对齐两个层面协同缓解了CST规模化的数据与合规瓶颈，验证了LLM在遵循认知刺激协议前提下的共情陪伴可行性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Cognitive Stimulation Therapy (CST) offers non-pharmacological support for elders with cognitive impairment, yet scalability remains constrained by reliance on trained facilitators and severe data scarcity, particularly for privacy-sensitive, low-resource languages such as Cantonese. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automated companionship, they often struggle to balance empathetic engagement with adherence to cognitive stimulation guidelines. We propose a framework addressing these challenges along two complementary axes. First, STaR-CS (Style-Transfer and Role-Conditioned Cognitive Stimulation) synthesizes multi-party dialogues through facilitator style modeling and structured skeleton extraction, mitigating data barriers. Building upon this corpus, the Reflective Cognitive Alignment (RCA) framework models stimulation interactions as a sequential decision process, integrating Protocol-Constrained Chain-of-Cognition (PC-CoC) for structured reasoning and Inference-Time Value Alignment (IVA) for principled response selection based on safety and engagement goals. Evaluations across six backbone LLMs and two independent judges show that RCA consistently improves protocol adherence, safety, and group facilitation over standard prompting baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/jiangjyjy/RCA_Agent.

</details>


### [103] [Relation Before Entity: Deferred Commitment in Language Model Factual Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17537)
*Divyansh Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究事实回忆过程中"关系类型信息"与"实体信息"在最终token位置变为因果活跃的深度时序差异，发现关系信息比实体信息更早控制生成，存在稳健的时间不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探究在事实回忆（recall）过程中，关系类型信息（如 capital-of）和实体特定信息（如 France 对应 Paris）是否在相同的网络深度、在最终token位置变为因果活跃。澄清二者时序关系有助于理解事实知识在语言模型中的激活与路由机制。

Method: 采用四种互补的因果诊断方法，覆盖四种 decoder-only 模型和八个 prompt 家族，测量关系信息与实体信息在最终token位置变为"生成控制性"的深度，并对全组合（16组模型-阈值）进行验证。

Result: 发现稳健的时间不对称性：关系信息先于实体信息获得生成控制权。在阈值0.4下，关系信息起点比实体信息早10-16层（约占网络深度的31-44%），且在阈值0.2-0.5范围内全部16种模型-阈值组合中该顺序均成立。关键的是，实体信息并非在早期缺失——早期层中实体token patching成功率达90-100%，只是实体对生成的"承诺"被推迟：实体信息在实体token位置已可获得，但只有在被路由至最终token后才变为生成控制性。

Conclusion: 关系信息与实体信息在因果激活时序上存在非对称性：关系信息更早控制生成，而实体信息虽早已可得，但其对生成的控制依赖于被路由至最终token位置后才实现。这表明事实回忆并非信息的简单同时激活，而涉及分层的时序路由机制。

Abstract: We ask whether relation-type information (e.g., capital-of) and entity-specific information (e.g., France to Paris) become causally active at the final-token position at the same depth during recall. Using four complementary causal diagnostics across four decoder-only models and eight prompt families, we find a robust temporal asymmetry: relation information becomes generation-controlling before entity information does. Relation onset precedes entity onset by 10-16 tested layers (31-44% of network depth) at threshold 0.4, with the ordering holding across all 16 model-threshold combinations for thresholds 0.2-0.5. Critically, entity information is not absent early: entity-token patching succeeds at 90-100% in early layers. Instead, entity commitment to generation is deferred: entity information is available at the entity-token position but becomes generation-controlling at the final token only after being routed there.

</details>


### [104] [From Pixels to Pairs: A Comprehensive Benchmark of LLM-Based Key-Value Extraction in Noisy Document Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17538)
*Zahra Anvari,Vassilis Athitsos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文对开源指令微调LLM在KV P抽取任务上进行了系统性基准测试，发现干净文本下模型表现接近有监督布局感知系统，但在OCR噪声下性能显著下降、模型间差距缩小，OCR质量成为主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于文档结构化信息抽取，但其在真实OCR噪声下的行为尚不清楚，现有评估多为干净文本，与实际部署存在差距。

Method: 在FUNSD、CORD、SROIE三个基准上，用Gold标注文本及PaddleOCR、EasyOCR、Tesseract的OCR输出，评估Gemma、Mistral、Qwen2.5、LLaMA 3、DeepSeek等解码器-only模型，采用统一评估协议隔离输入质量、模型设计与提示的影响。

Result: 高质量文本下LLM是强语义抽取器，部分接近有监督布局感知系统；OCR噪声下性能大幅下降，模型差距随输入损坏加剧而收窄；性能由文本语义推理与OCR噪声下文本保真度两因素决定；大模型在干净文本上的增益在噪声下递减，OCR质量成为主导；出现键值错位、幻觉、数值损坏等失败模式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了干净文本评估与真实部署之间的鸿沟，需协同提升OCR质量、结构化推理与LLM语义建模能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for structured information extraction from documents, yet their behavior under realistic OCR noise remains poorly understood.
